## 🤔 What is Seedance 2.0?

**Seedance 2.0** is a video generation model developed by **ByteDance**. It is the industry's first model supporting **simultaneous quad-modal input** — image, video, audio, and text.

**Key Features:**

* 🎥 **Text-to-Video** — Generate videos from text descriptions
* 🖼️ **Image-to-Video** — Animate static images into dynamic videos
* 📹 **Video-to-Video** — Transform and extend existing videos
* 🎵 **Audio-Driven** — Generate videos driven by audio input
* 📐 **Up to 1080p resolution**, 4–15 seconds duration
* 🔊 **Auto dubbing & scoring** — Automatic voiceover and background music

### PART 1 • 0:00–0:15 — THE REVEAL & THE KISS (9:16)

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
PART 1 • 0:00–0:15 — THE REVEAL & THE KISS (9:16)

0:00–0:03 — Arrival
Continuous crane arc already in motion around the couple on the tip of Tokyo Tower's antenna mast. Only one Tokyo Tower visible. Wind moves Jessy's hair and trench dress. He wears the yellow kawaii mascot helmet.
Jessy: "You're seriously wearing that up here?"
Man: "Only for you."

0:03–0:06 — Reveal
Camera continues the same arc. He removes the helmet, revealing tousled brown hair and blue eyes.

0:06–0:09 — Pull
Continuous crane widens. He pulls Jessy close by the waist, she grabs his jacket collar. The Polloai flag becomes visible above.

0:06–0:09 — Kiss
Camera keeps pulling back with a subtle Dutch roll. They kiss on the tip of Tokyo Tower as the grade transitions from hazy news footage to warm cinematic.

0:12–0:15 — Sway
Wide shot. They slowly turn while kissing. Tokyo Tower and the Polloai flag remain clearly visible.

PART 2 • 0:15–0:25 — THE SPIN & THE VIEW (9:16)

Continuity: First frame must exactly match the last frame of Part 1.

0:15–0:18 — Spin
Continue the identical crane arc and orbit. He spins Jessy once on the tower tip. Dress, jewelry and blue-streaked hair flow naturally.
Jessy: "Don't drop me from up here!"

0:18–0:21 — Second Kiss
Orbit tightens into a close two-shot. They share another soft kiss, foreheads touching between kisses.

0:21–0:23 — View
Camera cranes upward and pulls back. They face the glowing Tokyo skyline together with the Polloai flag above.

0:23–0:25 — Hold
Slow push-in. They remain embracing on the tip of Tokyo Tower until fade to black.

Strict: Maintain the exact reference camera movement throughout. All actions happen only on the tip of Tokyo Tower, with only one Tokyo Tower visible in every shot.
```

### VIDEO PROMPT — THE GHOST IN THE CARNIVAL (30s Horror-Comedy Vlog Short, 16:9) —…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
VIDEO PROMPT — THE GHOST IN THE CARNIVAL (30s Horror-Comedy Vlog Short, 16:9) — ROLES SWITCHED
Style: Found footage horror • paranormal investigation vlog • cinematic suspense • horror-comedy twist. Setting: abandoned carnival at night.

CHARACTER DESCRIPTIONS
Maya (the vlogger):
Early-20s girl, radiant and striking. Wears a flowing rainbow ombré dress — fabric transitioning from violet at the shoulders through blue, green, yellow, orange, to red at the hem, catching light with a subtle iridescent shimmer. Hair styled in the longer side braided into a thick rope braid streaked in matching rainbow pastel colors, small silver hoop earrings, soft glowing skin, bright hazel eyes with a curious, fearless sparkle. Carries a handheld camera and flashlight throughout. Same dress, hair, and gear consistent across every shot — no drift.
Jake (the boyfriend / "the ghost"):
Mid-20s, tall athletic build, tousled dark brown hair, strong jawline, warm brown eyes, light stubble, handsome and charming. Wears a fitted black bomber jacket over a grey henley, dark jeans, worn leather boots. Same face and outfit consistent across all appearances.
Setting: Abandoned carnival at night — rusted ferris wheel silhouette against the moon, cracked funhouse mirrors, tattered circus tents flapping in wind, broken carousel horses frozen mid-gallop, flickering string lights barely holding power.
Sound design: Low ambient horror BGM (creaking metal, distant wind chimes, faint off-key carousel melody) building tension throughout, swelling into a sharp orchestral stab at the jumpscare, then cutting to warm acoustic guitar strum for the comedic ending.

CUT-SCENE BREAKDOWN
(0:00–0:04) — ACT 1: The Investigation
Found-footage handheld shot: Maya walks alone through the carnival gates, flashlight beam cutting through fog, camera slightly shaky, rainbow braid swaying. Rusted ferris wheel looms behind her, carousel horses frozen in the dark.
Dialogue (Maya, to camera): "Alright guys… this place has been closed for like fifteen years. Let's see what we find."
BGM: low ambient drone, distant creaking metal.
(0:04–0:08) — ACT 2: The Warning Signs
Maya walks past a row of cracked funhouse mirrors. In one mirror's reflection — barely visible in the background — a flash of a dark jacket flickers past, gone before she turns. She doesn't notice. Camera lingers a beat too long on the empty mirror.
BGM: single high string note, tension rising.
(0:08–0:12) — ACT 3: The Reveal
Maya pushes into a tattered circus tent. Flashlight flickers. A pale face flashes directly behind her in frame — gone in a blink. She spins around, flashlight sweeping, rainbow dress catching the light.
Dialogue (Maya, nervous): "...Okay, who's there?"
BGM: dissonant sting.
(0:12–0:16) — ACT 4: The Chase
The figure appears directly behind Maya — full jumpscare, dark silhouette catching the light for a split second before vanishing. Maya bolts, camera shaking violently as she sprints between carousel horses, the figure flashing briefly in and out of frame from different angles.
Dialogue (Maya, panicked): "Oh my god— OH MY GOD—"
BGM: sharp orchestral hit, frantic percussion.
(0:16–0:20) — ACT 5: The False Ending
Maya ducks into the old ticket booth, slams the door shut behind her. Silence. Heavy breathing. She slides down against the door, flashlight shaking in her hand, camera catching her relieved exhale, braid falling loose over her shoulder.
Dialogue (Maya, whispering): "Okay... okay, I think I lost it."
BGM: music drops to near-silence, just her breathing and wind outside.
(0:20–0:25) — ACT 6: The Twist Begins
Behind Maya, unnoticed, a silhouette rises slowly in the booth's shadowed corner — tall frame stepping forward silently, closer, closer. Maya senses it, turns her flashlight up in dread.
BGM: tension rebuilding, single sustained note.
(0:25–0:30) — The Reveal & Comedy Twist
The "ghost" steps into the light — it's Jake, grinning, hands raised in mock surrender. Maya freezes in shock, then bursts out laughing in relief, swatting his arm.
Dialogue (Maya): "IS THAT YOU?! Jake, what the— I almost had a heart attack!"
Dialogue (Jake, laughing): "You scream like a little girl, you know that?"
BGM: sudden shift to warm, playful acoustic guitar strum. Hard cut to black.
Text overlay: "Never bring your boyfriend to a horror investigation."
```

### This is handheld documentary footage recorded on an early-2000s consumer DV…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
This is handheld documentary footage recorded on an early-2000s consumer DV camcorder by someone standing in a forest watching a boy try to capture a strange creature. The footage feels like real, imperfect home video of an unexpected wildlife encounter.

A boy around 12 years old, wearing a red cap, blue jacket and shorts, is in a dense forest clearing. He is very excited and determined. In front of him, attached to a tree trunk at about eye level, is a large, green, cocoon-like creature with a hard segmented shell, small visible eyes and slight twitching movements. The creature looks completely real, like an unknown deep-forest insect or animal.

The boy throws a small round object (like a ball) toward the creature while shouting encouragement. The object bounces off the hard green shell. The creature twitches and makes a small defensive movement. The boy picks up the object, adjusts his stance and throws again with more force. His friends can be heard shouting in the background. The boy looks thrilled when the creature reacts.

The camera, held by one of the friends, moves unsteadily to follow the action. It shakes a lot when the boy throws and when the creature twitches. There are quick natural cuts between the boy’s face full of excitement, the creature on the tree, and the thrown object hitting the shell.

The handheld camera shows constant shake, imperfect and drifting framing, frequent autofocus hunting, lens breathing, motion blur during throws, exposure changes between sunlight filtering through trees and shaded areas, and all the typical imperfections of an old DV camcorder. The person filming is clearly excited and moving around to get a better view.

Natural sound only: forest ambience (birds, wind in leaves), the boy shouting excitedly, the sound of the thrown object hitting the hard shell, footsteps on leaves and twigs, and distant voices of other kids. No music. No sound design.

The result must feel like authentic, raw home video of a group of kids in the forest encountering and trying to capture a bizarre real creature, captured on an old DV camcorder.
```

### An army supply depot at night, rows of fuel tanks and trucks under bright…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
An army supply depot at night, rows of fuel tanks and trucks under bright floodlights. A slimy alien with long tentacles has wrapped itself around a fuel truck's hose line, and the fuel is leaking onto the ground. No one can shoot near it — one spark could blow up the whole depot. A technician who works on the depot's water systems every day is at the sprinkler control box. A soldier stands ready nearby with a length of live wire. Sound: hissing fuel, dripping water, alien hissing, shouting, radio voices.

Shot 1 (0–3s) — THE HOOK: Low shot along the fuel line. The alien's tentacles are wrapped tight around the hose, squeezing it, fuel spraying out onto the ground below. Soldiers back away with their rifles lowered — too dangerous to fire here. One shouts into his radio: "NOBODY SHOOT! IT'LL BLOW THE WHOLE YARD!"

Shot 2 (3–6s): At the sprinkler control box, the technician grabs the big lever, watching the fuel spread across the yard. He calls on the radio: "Flooding it now! Get the wire ready!" Nearby, a soldier holds a long live wire, waiting for his signal.

Shot 3 (6–9s): The technician pulls the lever. Sprinklers burst on across the whole yard, water pouring down and washing the spilled fuel away, leaving the ground soaked but safe. The alien, still wrapped on the hose, is now standing in a wide flooded puddle, alone in the open water.

Shot 4 (9–13s) — BIG SLOW-MOTION MOMENT: The soldier throws the live wire into the flooded ground. Bullet time — the wire spins through the air in slow motion, water droplets hanging around it, sparks already jumping off the tip — and it lands in the puddle right beside the alien.

Shot 5 (13–15s): Time snaps back. Electricity rips across the flooded ground in a bright flash, lighting up the alien from every side as it shakes and drops. The fuel truck sits safe and untouched. Soldiers watch from a distance as the sparks die down. Cut.
```

Main Subject:
Young Korean woman, early 20s, delivery helmet with visor up, windbreaker uniform jacket, fingerless gloves, focused determined energy, quick reflexes.
Location:
Narrow winding hillside streets in a Korean city at dusk, delivery route. Steep alleys, glowing streetlamps flickering on, scooters parked along walls, distant city lights below. No heavy traffic.
Visual Style:
Fast-paced realistic documentary tone, urgent urban energy, dusky transitional lighting.
Camera Style:
Early 2010s action-mounted camcorder feel but handheld, heavy shake from bike vibration, motion blur on turns, exposure shifting rapidly between streetlights and shadow. No stabilization.
Timeline (15 sec, each slot = 2 compressed beats):
00:00–00:03 → She revs the scooter, then speeds off down a steep alley, engine whining.
00:03–00:06 → She leans hard into a sharp turn, then narrowly avoids a parked bicycle, laughing nervously after.
00:06–00:09 → She glances at a delivery bag on the back seat, then mutters "늦으면 안 되는데!" ("I really can't be late!").
00:09–00:12 → She speeds up a final hill, then screeches to a stop in front of a doorway.
00:12–00:15 → She grabs the bag, running to the door, then turns back grinning "배달 완료!" ("Delivery complete!"), as the scene cuts.
Audio:
Engine revving, wind rushing past, tires skidding, distant city dusk ambience. Her dialogue as noted above. No music.
Goal: A fast, urgent delivery dash thrilling, focused, alive with motion.

画角を補正しない。

魚眼レンズ特有の歪み、近接パース、顔や手が大きく見える遠近感を最後まで維持する。

カメラは超広角魚眼レンズを使用した固定カメラ。

映像全体を通してカメラ位置は維持する。

キャラクターのみがレンズへ近付いたり離れたりする。

レンズの歪み、近接パース、遠近感は最後まで維持する。

観客に向けたステージダンスではなく、レンズ一台に向かって全力でアイドルパフォーマンスをするセルフィーMV。

キャラクターの顔、髪型、髪色、瞳、衣装、体型、雰囲気、デザインはファーストフレームから絶対に変えない。

キャラクターを別人化しない。

衣装を変更しない。

映像スタイル：

高品質な日本アニメMV。

滑らかな作画。

表情豊か。

魚眼レンズの迫力を活かした近距離パフォーマンス。

音楽：

明るくキャッチーな日本語アイドルソング。

BPM高め。

可愛い女性ボーカル。

短く覚えやすいサビ。

電子音を含むポップなアイドル楽曲。

歌詞：

「ねぇ！

こっち見て！

今日はキミだけ

特等席

ドキドキ

止まらない

だ・い・す・き！」

タイムライン：

【0.0〜1.5秒】

ファーストフレームと完全一致して開始。

キャラクターがレンズを覗き込み、少しだけ目を近づける。

魚眼で瞳が大きく迫って見える。

歌詞：
「ねぇ！」

【1.5〜3.0秒】

キャラクターがカメラに向かって人差し指を差す。

指が魚眼レンズで大きく誇張される。

小さく笑う。

歌詞：
「こっち見て！」

【3.0〜5.0秒】

リズムに合わせて左右に揺れる。

歌詞：
「今日はキミだけ」

【5.0〜7.0秒】

キャラクターがレンズにさらに近づく。

片目が画面いっぱいに近づき、ウインクする。

可愛く挑発するような表情。

歌詞：
「特等席」

【7.0〜9.0秒】

両手でレンズの周囲を囲み、ファーストフレームのフレームポーズを変形させる。

指が手前で大きく見える。

歌詞：
「ドキドキ」

【9.0〜11.0秒】

レンズを軽くトントンと叩く。

画面が小さく揺れる。

レンズ接触による一瞬の軽いブレを入れる。

歌詞：
「止まらない」

【11.0〜13.5秒】

キャラクターが笑顔で顔を近づけ、カメラ目線で小さく踊る。

肩、頭、手だけを使った近距離アイドルダンス。

全身ダンスにはしない。

歌詞：
「だ・い・す・き！」

【13.5〜15.0秒】

最後にキャラクターが手のひらでレンズを覆う。

画面が魚眼のまま暗くなり、ブラックアウトして終了。

禁止事項：

カメラ切り替え禁止。

通常レンズ化禁止。

魚眼歪みの補正禁止。

遠景の全身ダンス禁止。

ステージ俯瞰禁止。

キャラクターの顔や衣装の変更禁止。

別人化禁止。

実写化禁止。

背景の大幅変更禁止。

歌詞を英語にしない。

字幕を表示しない。
```

The camera glides between the giant trees.

Tiny floating seed-like organisms drift gracefully through the air, changing color as they move. Every plant reacts subtly as the camera passes, opening luminous flowers and releasing glowing spores that slowly rise toward the sky.

The entire biosphere behaves like one interconnected living organism.

The camera slowly retreats into orbit.

The magnificent planet rotates peacefully beneath three moons while glowing migration paths pulse gently across its surface.

The final frame lingers on the breathtaking world drifting silently through the stars before fading into darkness.

Soft colorful sunrise. Bioluminescent forests. Crystal reflections. Glowing rivers. Warm atmospheric haze. Gentle orbital sunlight. High dynamic range. Physically inspired volumetric lighting.

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Crown Prince Naruhito & Masako Owada wedding (1993) — A royal wedding watched by millions, the imperial motorcade through Tokyo, crowds lining the streets waving flags.

Seedance 2 creation

This is handheld documentary footage recorded on an early-2000s consumer DV camcorder from the sidewalk along the imperial procession route through Tokyo on June 9, 1993. The footage feels like real, imperfect home video of a famous royal wedding day.

The recording shows a packed street in central Tokyo. Thousands of people line both sides of the road, many holding small Japanese flags, some standing on tiptoes, some holding up children to see over the crowd. Police officers in white gloves keep the crowd behind barricades. The atmosphere is calm but electric — people craning their necks, murmuring to each other, checking watches. Elderly women in formal kimono stand patiently. Salarymen in suits have stepped out of nearby office buildings. Students sit on curbs with lunchboxes. Some people hold disposable cameras and early camcorders of their own.

The camera moves along the crowd trying to find a good viewing spot, catching glimpses between heads and raised flags. It captures close-ups of faces — a mother lifting her daughter onto her shoulders, an old man wiping his eyes, a group of office workers whispering excitedly. A police motorcycle passes first. Then another. The crowd begins to stir and push forward against the barricades.

The imperial motorcade appears in the distance. Black limousines with Imperial Guards on motorcycles lead the way. Then the main car — an open-top imperial vehicle — comes into view. Crown Prince Naruhito is visible in formal morning dress, and beside him Masako Owada in a simple white dress and tiara, both composed and faintly smiling. The crowd erupts — flags wave, people bow their heads slightly, some clap, some call out. The car moves slowly. For a few seconds the couple is clearly visible through the gap in the crowd before the car passes and the view is lost behind raised hands and flags.

The camera pushes through the crowd trying to keep the car in frame, catching a final glimpse as the motorcade turns the corner and disappears. The crowd lingers, talking among themselves, some reviewing footage on their own camcorder flip-out screens. The street slowly returns to normal traffic.

The handheld camera shows natural shake, drifting framing, autofocus issues as it tries to focus on the moving motorcade through a forest of raised flags and hands, lens breathing, and reactive zooming as the person filming fights for the best view in a dense crowd. There are natural cuts between wide shots of the street, close-ups of the crowd waiting, the motorcade approaching, the moment the couple is visible, and the aftermath as the crowd disperses.

Natural sound only: the murmur of thousands of voices, police whistles, distant motorcycle engines, the crowd erupting in cheers and applause as the motorcade passes, flags rustling, children calling out, the low rumble of the imperial vehicle. No music added.

The result must feel like authentic, raw footage of someone standing in the crowd on the day Crown Prince Naruhito married Masako Owada, captured on an old DV camcorder.
```

Close-up of Mio staring upward with huge innocent blue eyes, pretending he did nothing wrong.

No subtitle.

[00:03.0 – 00:04.0]
SHOT 4 — Gentle Warning
Lila kneels beside Mio and raises one finger, gently warning him.

[00:04.0 – 00:05.0]

Mio turns away from Lila, swishes his fluffy tail, and walks toward the flower bed as if he is not listening.

[00:05.0 – 00:06.2]

Mio jumps into the flower bed, scattering petals and leaves. Lila gasps and freezes with surprise.

[00:06.2 – 00:07.4]

Lila stands firmer and gives a clear stop gesture with her hand. She looks more serious now, but still caring.

English subtitle:
"Mio! Not there!"
Camera: medium shot with strong line of action between Lila and Mio.

[00:07.4 – 00:08.4]
SHOT 8 — Offended Kitten
Close-up of Mio crouching low. His ears fold back, his eyes narrow, and his white fur begins to rise. He looks offended and angry.
Audio: low irritated cat growl.
No subtitle.
Camera: close-up push-in on Mio’s face.

[00:08.4 – 00:09.8]

Blue magical sparks flicker around Mio. His fur spikes up, his body expands, and the garden air pulses around him. Lila steps back in surprise.
Audio: rising magical energy, small angry cat snarl.
No subtitle.
Camera: dramatic medium push-in.

[00:09.8 – 00:11.4]

Monster Mio fully appears: huge fluffy white body, spiky fur, glowing cyan-blue eyes, large claws, and a cute-but-scary angry expression. He leans toward Lila and roars.

[00:11.4 – 00:12.8]
SHOT 11 — Lila Frightened
Lila jumps backward in fear, drops the watering can, and stares at monster Mio with wide shocked eyes. The watering can hits the stone path and rolls slightly.

[00:12.8 – 00:15.0]
SHOT 12 — Cute Reversal
Monster Mio suddenly loses his scary energy. He sneezes, shrinks instantly, and returns to tiny fluffy white kitten form. Mio sits innocently again as if nothing happened. Lila slowly relaxes, still shocked and confused.
Audio:

Mio soft meow: 「にゃあ」
No subtitle for animal sounds.

English subtitle:

Camera: medium final shot, soft gentle settle. End with Mio blinking innocently.

Autofocus briefly hunts again. 00:12–00:15 The camera lags behind as he walks toward home. He glances back with a genuine smile for just a second before looking forward again. The recording ends abruptly mid-step as though the camcorder was switched off without warning. Audio: Natural ambient sound only — morning birds, distant lawn mower, soft wind, leaves rustling, faint neighborhood chatter, footsteps on pavement, bicycle chain noises, occasional dog barking far away. No music. No narration. No sound design. Goal: Create a fictional, deeply believable "found MiniDV family tape" from summer 2003 that feels like an ordinary memory accidentally preserved on an old Sony Handycam. ### 以未经处理的iPhone竖屏手持拍摄的真实视频素材风格呈现，所有相机设置均为自动，无任何后期调色或特效。画面带有真实的手持轻微抖动和操作者呼吸感，自动对焦偶尔出…

#### 📝 Prompt

```
以未经处理的iPhone竖屏手持拍摄的真实视频素材风格呈现，所有相机设置均为自动，无任何后期调色或特效。画面带有真实的手持轻微抖动和操作者呼吸感，自动对焦偶尔出现搜索、延迟和短暂失焦，自动白平衡在办公室自然光与顶灯间自然切换。图像整体平坦，保留真实的镜头光晕、边缘色差、轻微过曝、运动模糊等光学瑕疵。仅使用画内自然环境音效（空调低频嗡鸣、椅子轻微摩擦、远处办公室环境声），麦克风说话时可能出现轻微压缩失真。采用第一人称面试官视角（手部偶尔可见），镜头运动为自然反应式。 0-2秒：中景平视，手持镜头轻微抖动固定拍摄办公室内。一名年轻女子穿着和黑色带点过膝吊带丝袜端坐在办公桌前，双手交叠放在桌上，表情平静。前景可见面试官的手、简历和一部蓝色手机。背景是简约办公室，玻璃隔断和百叶窗。男生低沉严肃画外音：“给我一个必须录用你的理由。”自动对焦在女生脸上轻微搜索。 2-5秒：女子突然起身，快速抓起桌上第一人称这边的一部黑色苹果17手机，用力向旁边地上甩出。镜头因惊讶本能快速下摇跟拍，出现明显抖动和构图不稳。手机撞击浅色木质柜子后重重摔在白色瓷砖地面上，零件和外壳散落。自动对焦激烈搜索，画面短暂失焦后拉回地面碎裂细节。巨大撞击声和碎裂弹跳声清晰，麦克风轻微失真。男生疑问的画外音说：“唉！你这是干啥？” 5-10秒：镜头快速抬升回到初始构图，女子重新坐好，双手交叠放回桌上，脸上带着自信且略带俏皮的微笑，眨巴眼睛，然后眼神直视镜头（面试官）。桌面上的手机已消失，只剩下纸质的应聘文件夹。女生甜美自信地说：“从我下个月工资里扣。”环境白噪音恢复，画面轻微手持呼吸抖动，自动白平衡轻微调整。画面呈现出真实的未经处理手持视频质感，纪录片级别的自然不完美感，无任何后期调色或特效。所有相机行为均符合iPhone/手机自动拍摄的物理特性。千万记住扔出的手机不是女角色自己的手机
```

### # 制作方針

![日本語](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-日本語-green)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
# 制作方針
高品質な日本のロボットアニメーションスタイル、シネマティック照明、滑らかで重厚感のある動き、リアルな物理演算、字幕なし。

# 参照素材
- Image 1: ロボットの3面図。この画像を厳密に参照してロボットのデザイン、プロポーション、カラーリングの一貫性を保つ。
- Image 2: ビームライフル参照画像。画像左側がビームを撃ったシーン、画像右側が真横からの構図。ロボットが右手に持つライフルのデザインと描写をこの画像に厳密に従わせる。
- Image 3: コクピット内部参照画像。画像左側が座席からの視点、画像右側が斜め前からの構図。コクピット内部のレイアウトとディテールをこの画像に厳密に従わせる。
- Image 4: 搭乗少女のバストアップ。この画像を厳密に参照して顔（目・鼻・口・輪郭・髪型）の形状と位置関係を一致させる。
- Image 5: 搭乗少女の3面図。体型とパイロットスーツの一貫性を保つ。
- Image 6: 軍事基地の環境参照画像。背景と全体的な雰囲気はこの画像に従う。
- Image 7: 格納庫前でのロボットの位置・構図参照。位置、大きさ、スケール感、構図の参照に主に使用。ロボットのデザインはImage 1を優先して一貫性を保つ。
- Movie 1: 飛び立つ動作参照動画。動きのタイミング、バーニア噴射、カメラワーク、建物との位置関係のみ使用。
- Movie 2: ドローン撃墜動作参照動画。動きのタイミング、ビーム射出、爆発、カメラワーク、位置関係のみ使用。

# シーン構成
Shot 1 (2秒): 
Image 7の構図とカメラワークを厳密に参照。軍事基地でロボットが格納庫から前方へ重厚に歩み出てくる。右手にはビームライフルを構えている。Image 7と同じカメラ位置からゆっくりロボットへズームインしていく。

Shot 2 (3秒): 
コクピット内部。少女の顔の正面クローズアップから、カメラがゆっくり後ろへ回り込む。少女は操作パネルを操作した後、レバーを握り、足元のペダルを強く踏み込む。常に無表情でジト目、笑わない。自然なまばたき。

Shot 3 (2秒): 
Movie 1のカメラワークと動きを厳密に参照。ロボットが軽くしゃがんだ後、背面と脚部のバーニアを勢いよく噴射して飛び立つ。Movie 1と同じカメラアングルと動きで、重厚で迫力のある離陸。離陸時にカメラが振動する。

Shot 4 (2秒): 
コクピット内部のダイナミックな連続クローズアップ。足元（ペダルを踏み込む）→ 手元（レバー操作）→ 顔のアップ。少女は無表情のジト目で「はずさない」と小さくつぶやく。声は若い少女の落ち着いたクールなトーン。軽く汗をかき、自然なまばたきと息遣い。

Shot 5 (3秒): 
Movie 2のカメラワークとタイミングを厳密に参照。ロボットがライフルを構え、派手なイエローのビームを射出。Movie 2と同じ構図とカメラ移動で、ビームは粒子と衝撃波をまき散らし、飛行中の軍事ドローンに命中させて大爆発。ドローンは半壊しながら落下する。迫力のVFX。

Shot 6 (2秒): 
画面を2分割。左側に少女の顔アップ、右側にロボットの頭部アップ。少女は軽く汗をかいて息を少し乱し、無表情のジト目で「当たった…」とつぶやく。声は若い少女のクールで少し緊張したトーン。ゆっくりズームアウト。
```

### @image1 — Haruto,27岁,建筑师,身高185cm,黑色微乱短发,银色项链坠饰,黑色高领衫/衬衫,黑色西裤,黑色靴子,性格沉稳睿智。

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
@image1 — Haruto,27岁,建筑师,身高185cm,黑色微乱短发,银色项链坠饰,黑色高领衫/衬衫,黑色西裤,黑色靴子,性格沉稳睿智。
@image2 — Aoi,24岁,数字艺术家,身高168cm,红色长发刘海,项圈搭配吊坠,黑色oversize西装外套,黑色百褶裙,黑色靴子,性格活泼富有表现力,喜欢城市夜晚。
场景:涩谷十字路口 / 道顿堀(夜晚,霓虹灯闪烁,人潮涌动,充满活力的都市夜景氛围)。
第一段(0-10秒)
风格:日系动漫电影质感,高对比度霓虹光效,深蓝夜色基调,雨后湿润路面反光,运镜流畅带丝滑转场。
0-3秒 — 广角建立镜头:
镜头从高空俯冲而下,穿过涩谷十字路口璀璨的霓虹广告牌,降落跟随 @image1 与 @image2 并肩走过斑马线,Aoi手臂轻摆,笑着东张西望。
对白(Aoi,兴奋):
「ねえ、ハルト、あそこの看板見て!めっちゃ大きくない?」
3-6秒 — 侧向平行跟拍:
镜头从侧面平视高度跟随,Haruto转头看向她指的方向,霓虹灯光在他脸上投射出蓝紫色光影,嘴角浮现淡淡笑意。
对白(Haruto,沉稳):
「ああ、確かに。でも君の方が目立ってると思うよ。」
6-10秒 — 反应特写:
镜头特写Aoi略带娇羞又忍不住笑出声的表情,轻轻捶了一下Haruto的手臂,画面平滑拉远至广角,两人在闪烁霓虹与人潮中并肩而笑。
第二段(10-20秒)
10-13秒 — 低角度行走镜头:
镜头低角度跟随两人的脚步,Aoi俏皮地小跳一下模仿模特走姿,Haruto无奈摇头轻笑。
对白(Aoi,俏皮):
「見て見て、モデルみたいでしょ?」
13-16秒 — 环绕镜头:
镜头围绕两人半圈旋转,他们在一处霓虹招牌前短暂停留,五彩灯光在两人脸上流转变幻。
对白(Haruto,忍笑):
「うん、まるでファッション誌の表紙みたいだ。」
16-20秒 — 双人中景镜头:
镜头缓缓靠近,两人继续前行,Aoi调皮地用肩膀撞了撞Haruto,镜头平滑摇向夜空中闪烁的高楼灯光与霓虹广告。
第三段(20-30秒)
20-23秒 — 逆光剪影镜头:
镜头采用逆光角度,两人并肩而行的剪影在璀璨都市灯海中清晰勾勒,街灯与霓虹交织闪烁。
对白(Aoi,温柔):
「今日、一緒に歩けて楽しかった。」
23-26秒 — 双人侧脸特写:
镜头从侧面捕捉两人转头相视而笑的画面,夜风轻拂过Aoi的红发与Haruto的黑发,霓虹色光在他们脸颊上跳跃闪烁。
对白(Haruto,真诚):
「うん、俺も。また今度、どこか行こう。」
26-30秒 — 广角收尾镜头:
镜头缓缓拉远至广角,两人并肩走入璀璨夜色与人潮之中,城市霓虹灯光渐次点亮整个画面,镜头画面缓缓淡出收尾。
镜头技巧总览:广角建立镜头、侧向平行跟拍、反应特写、低角度行走镜头、环绕镜头、双人中景镜头、逆光剪影镜头、双人侧脸特写、广角收尾镜头——每3-4秒切换一种运镜方式,配合流畅转场(叠化/匹配剪辑)保持叙事节奏自然连贯。
光效强化:霓虹灯光反射、深蓝夜色基调、湿润路面倒影、逆光剪影轮廓、多彩广告牌流光溢彩、脸部特写时的柔和霓虹光晕。
配音一致性:Aoi声音活泼轻快带点俏皮感;Haruto声音沉稳低沉,偶尔带点温柔的反击语气——全程日语对白自然流畅,背景音为都市夜晚人潮声、远处霓虹广告电流声与微风声。
负面提示词(需避免):禁止角色脸部/身体比例前后不一致、禁止转场生硬跳切、禁止灯光过曝导致细节丢失、禁止口型与对白不同步、禁止背景霓虹招牌文字扭曲变形。
```

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Style: 8K photorealistic — real phone-and-mirrorless vlog footage, no 3D render, no game engine, no anime.
Format: Modern K-pop idol YouTube behind-the-scenes vlog — handheld selfie energy, quick punchy cuts, whip-pans and snap-zooms between shots.
Lighting: Clean and flattering — vanity mirror bulb lights as key, soft white studio spill from the doorway, gentle bloom, K-beauty glow. High-key on the face, controlled elsewhere.
Color: Chic monochrome palette. 60:30:10 — soft warm gray / deep black wardrobe / soft pink accent pop.
Camera: Mix of front-facing phone selfie (slight wide-lens distortion, arm's-length) and handheld mirrorless. Natural micro-shake, quick reframes. 180-degree shutter motion blur.
Skin: Pore-level realism with dewy K-beauty finish — vellus hair, subtle blush flush, glossy lips, real catch-lights in the eyes.
Acting: Bubbly, natural idol-vlog energy — genuine smiles, playful micro-expressions, eye-contact with the lens, tiny laughs, chest rise from breathing. Never posed-frozen, always alive.
Physics: Gravity and inertia respected — long hair bounce and sway, pleated skirt movement, correct contact shadows. No floating props.
Composition: Centered selfie framing and rule-of-thirds handheld. Subject moving from frame one.
Continuity: Same idol RENA, same outfit, same makeup, same waiting room across every cut. No identity drift.
Technical: 24fps smooth motion, snappy vlog-paced editing, 8K detail, no jitter.
Audio: Upbeat cheerful room tone. RENA speaks short natural Korean one-liners as written in each cut — bright, youthful female voice, casual vlog delivery, accurate lip-sync. Light, bouncy vlog energy.

Characters:
RENA — 20 year old K-pop idol, 166cm, slim 7-head proportions. Adorable cute-type visual: soft round face with full cheeks, big round sparkling dark brown eyes with aegyo-sal, small nose, dewy glass skin, coral-pink gradient lips, tiny beauty mark on her left cheek. Very long straight black hair past her chest, center-parted with thin face-framing strands. Pearl-and-silver drop earrings in both ears. Outfit: black ribbed knit top with an oversized pleated collar and a black ribbon tie, white shirt collar peeking at the neckline, black micro pleated skirt with a side belt buckle, black crew socks, chunky black platform loafers.

Scene:
An idol waiting room at an MV shooting studio, daytime. A vanity mirror ringed with warm bulb lights sits camera-left; a rolling clothing rack of garment bags stands behind, a gray couch with her phone and a small pink standing fan camera-right. The door to the bright studio hallway is upstage. RENA is alone, fully in hair and makeup, killing time before her set call — filming a behind-the-scenes vlog on her phone. Fast, cheerful, handheld throughout.

CUT 1 (~2s) — Front-facing phone selfie, arm's length, slight wide-lens distortion:
RENA pops in close to the lens with a bright grin and a quick finger-heart — long black hair swinging in, eyes sparkling in the bulb light.
She says brightly: "하이 여러분~!" (Hi everyone~!)

CUT 2 (~2s) — Fast whip-pan across the waiting room, handheld POV:
Her hand flips the phone around — a whip across the bulb-lit vanity mirror, the rolling clothing rack of garment bags, the gray couch with the small pink standing fan. Whips back just as her face re-enters frame.
Her voice off-camera, playful: "여기는 대기실!" (This is the waiting room!)

CUT 3 (~2s) — Snap-zoom to the vanity mirror reflection, handheld:
She leans into the bulb-lit mirror, fixes one face-framing strand with two fingers, presses her coral lips together with a tiny satisfied nod.
She whispers to her reflection with a smile: "예쁘죠?" (Pretty, right?)

CUT 4 (~1.5s) — Macro insert, tight close-up, shallow focus:
Detail shot: her fingers straighten the black ribbon tie at the pleated collar, pearl-and-silver earring swaying in bokeh bulb light, white shirt collar crisp at the neckline. No dialogue — only soft fabric rustle.

CUT 5 (~2s) — Medium handheld, phone tracking her, center of the room:
She does one quick playful spin to show the MV outfit — micro pleated skirt flaring, long black hair fanning out — landing square to the lens with both hands framing her face.
She sings out as she lands: "짜잔~!" (Ta-da~!)

CUT 6 (~2s) — Snappy cut, handheld close on the couch:
She drops onto the gray couch, grabs a bottle of banana milk from the side table and sips through the straw, cheeks puffing slightly, eyebrows bouncing.
A happy little hum through the straw, then: "음~ 맛있어!" (Mmm~ so good!)

CUT 7 (~1.5s) — Quick punch-in, tight selfie from the couch:
She holds the banana milk bottle up beside her face like a commercial, taps it twice with one finger, flashes a cheeky closed-lip grin at the lens.
She says in a mock-CF voice: "이거 최고." (This one's the best.)

CUT 8 (~2s) — Arm's-length selfie sign-off:
A staff knock sounds off-screen — her eyes go wide with excitement. A fast wave, a big wink with a double finger-heart, and she springs up out of frame — camera holds on the bulb-lit mirror for a half-beat.
She calls out as she jumps up: "가자! 안녕~!" (Let's go! Bye~!)
```

A lovely Chinese woman waiting at the bus stop during a storm, almost getting wet.

Main Subject & Appearance:

Identity: A young Chinese woman in her early 20s, possessing a natural, unpretentious beauty and a warm, approachable demeanor.
Features: Soft, natural skin texture with no visible makeup. Her black, wavy hair is loosely tied back into a low, messy ponytail, with several slightly damp bangs clinging softly to her forehead and cheek due to the humidity.
Wardrobe: She wears an oversized, light heather-grey zip-up hoodie layered casually over a slightly faded, ribbed white tank top. Her lower half consists of relaxed-fit, light-wash blue jeans and scuffed black canvas sneakers.
Constraint: Her face, clothing, and overall identity must remain strictly consistent throughout the entire generation.

Environment & Atmosphere:

Location: A quiet, unassuming bus stop shelter in a small Chinese town during a steady, gentle summer rain. The shelter is simple, perhaps slightly weathered with a green metal or fiberglass roof.
Atmosphere: The air is visibly heavy with humid, misty summer air.
Surroundings: Wet, dark asphalt pavement gleaming with reflections of the muted, overcast sky. Deep puddles line the curb. The road is bordered by lush, green leafy trees swaying slightly in the breeze, concrete utility poles, and the faint, hazy silhouettes of modest apartment buildings in the deep background. An occasional scooter with a plastic rain poncho zips by. There is absolutely no commercial focus, no bright billboards, and no dense crowds.

Visual Style & Camera Treatment:

Aesthetic: Ultra-realistic observational documentary footage. The goal is a highly authentic, unscripted slice-of-life memory—as if a friend is casually recording her while they wait together.
Camera Specs: Mimics a high-quality consumer digital camera from the 2008–2012 era (e.g., an early Canon PowerShot or entry-level DSLR). High image resolution but lacking modern HDR. Flat, natural color reproduction with realistic, true-to-life skin tones.
Motion & Lens: Handheld but comfortable and mostly stable. The framing makes small, natural, breathing corrections. Include occasional, subtle autofocus hunting or minor exposure stepping when the light changes.
Negative Constraints: NO heavy "shaky-cam," NO exaggerated VHS/vintage degradation, NO cinematic teal-and-orange color grading, and NO obvious artificial camera effects. The rain should behave with perfect physical realism, not like a Hollywood rain machine.

Action & Sequence:

[00:00–00:02] The Rain: Standing just at the edge of the bus shelter. She casually extends her right hand out from under the roof, palm up. A few heavy raindrops land on the sleeve of her grey hoodie, immediately turning the fabric dark grey in small patches. She gives a small, peaceful smile.

[00:02–00:04] The Wait: She retreats a step and sits down on the worn shelter bench. A steady stream of rainwater drips from the edge of the shelter's roof in the foreground. She leans forward, resting her elbows near her knees, quietly observing the wet road.

[00:04–00:06] The Time Check: She pulls an early-2000s style silver flip phone from her pocket. She snaps it open; the pale screen casts a very faint, cool glow on her face. She sighs slightly, snaps the phone shut with a distinct click, and tucks it away.

[00:06–00:08] The False Hope: The hazy glow of headlights appears far down the wet road. Her posture immediately shifts—she stands up instinctively, her expression brightening with hopeful anticipation as she watches the approaching vehicle. The camera pans slightly to follow her movement.

[00:08–00:10] The Disappointment: A large transit bus passes right by the shelter without slowing down, its tires kicking up a light spray of water from the curb puddles. She watches it disappear into the mist, then lets out a quiet, genuine laugh at her own bad luck, her shoulders dropping in mild defeat.

[00:10–00:12] The Chill: The rain grows slightly heavier, the sound intensifying. She shivers subtly and grabs the lapels of her grey hoodie, pulling it tighter around herself. She brushes a wet strand of hair away from her eye and sits back down on the bench.

[00:12–00:15] The Connection: While looking down the road, she suddenly glances over and notices the camera pointing at her. A sheepish, warm smile breaks across her face. She shakes her head lightly in amusement, then turns her gaze back to the empty, rainy road to continue waiting. The camera lingers on her profile for a beat before the video ends.

Audio Design:

Strictly natural, in-scene diegetic sound. The continuous, soothing static of light summer rain hitting concrete. The hollow pitter-patter of water dripping from the shelter roof. The distant, whirring hum of a scooter passing on wet asphalt. The swish of car tires cutting through puddles. The rustling of wet leaves in the wind.
No music. No narration. No cinematic sound design. Pure realism.
```

A tense body-horror transformation at an archaeological expedition dig site in a vast sun-scorched desert at harsh midday, heat shimmer, drifting sand and dust.

The shot opens at extreme ground level on a SMALL black desert scorpion — only palm-sized, realistic natural scale — ALREADY sprinting at full speed across the sand from the very first frame, mid-run, never stationary, the camera tracking low and fast right beside it. It races across open ground, weaving past scattered expedition gear — brushes, trowels, open crates, ropes, dusty boots of crew members — a long fast ground-level chase.

Still in the same unbroken shot, the small scorpion reaches a man crouched at the dig — the man [IMAGE REFERENCE], in a dusty khaki expedition field shirt with rolled sleeves, utility vest, cargo pants and glasses. He holds a small ancient artifact up in both hands close to his face, calm and completely unaware, carefully examining and inspecting its fine details, turning it slowly to study the carvings, fully absorbed and fascinated by the small relic. The scorpion suddenly leaps onto the back of his hand. ONLY at the moment it lands does he react — he flinches hard, startled. The camera stays locked on the scorpion, following it closely as it scurries fast up his forearm, over his shoulder, onto his neck, and jabs its stinger hard into his neck.

Only now does the camera ease back slightly to a medium shot: he cries out, slaps the scorpion off and flings it away, stumbling to his feet, gripping his neck in pain and confusion. The shot stays close for the finale — a raw handheld medium shot, the camera shaking and reactive like real documentary footage, staying tight on the creature as it screeches and rampages through the dig site, thrashing its pincers and tail, knocking over crates and equipment, while terrified crew members in matching khaki outfits sprint past the camera in panic, dust kicking up, the handheld camera jostling among the chaos, gritty and real. No aerial view, no wide shot — stay in the chaos at ground level until the end. No music.

### SCENE CONTEXT

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
SCENE CONTEXT
An elegant young woman films herself on her phone across one evening out: a short front-camera selfie moment in a luxury hotel room, a mirror selfie in an elevator, a walk past a boutique window at dusk, and the longest part — a dim candlelit restaurant where she films her reflection in a wall mirror across the table. Casual self-admiring video diary, calm and confident.

ACTIVE REFERENCES
@image1: young woman, mid-20s, long brown wavy hair, black strapless corset dress with subtle textured shimmer, diamond tennis necklace, butterfly diamond stud earrings, soft glam makeup. 100% matches the reference. Same dress, hair and jewelry in every cut.

FORMAT MODE
Timed multishot, four segments, HARD CUT between each. Inside each segment: one continuous take, single steady framing, camera position held from the segment's first frame to its last.
0.0–2.5s — CUT 1 hotel room, front camera
2.5–5.5s — CUT 2 elevator mirror
5.5–9.0s — CUT 3 evening street, boutique window reflection
9.0–15.0s — CUT 4 restaurant, wall mirror across the table

OPTICS
All segments: 84° wide FOV, clean modern phone lens, deep focus, everything sharp. The lens renders the frame evenly — brightness and shadow come only from the scene's own lighting. No drift mid-segment.

CAMERA
Every cut is footage from her own phone held in her hand. Her arm is steady and relaxed: only a soft natural sway under 1 cm, smooth and continuous, the frame floats gently at one fixed framing per segment. CUT 2: mid-segment she does one quick digital punch zoom-in on her face, then back out. CUT 4: slow digital zoom-in toward her reflection over the last 2 seconds.

ACTION
CUT 1 — a brief selfie beat, phone out of frame, front camera at arm's length slightly above eye level: she stands in one spot in the hotel room, crystal chandelier above, holds her pose calmly, tilts her chin once, light closed-lip smile.
CUT 2 — elevator interior, warm metallic walls, she stands facing the elevator mirror holding the phone horizontally in both hands at chest height, phone visible in the reflection; she shifts weight to one hip, adjusts the necklace with two fingers, calm gaze into the mirror.
CUT 3 — evening street at dusk: she walks at 4 km/h parallel to a large lit boutique window on her left. She holds the phone at chest height in her right hand, screen toward her, lens pointed at the glass at a slight 45° angle ahead of her, and keeps this exact grip and angle for the whole segment. The frame shows the shop window: her sharp reflection walks through it alongside lit mannequins, the phone visible in her hand in the reflection, warm shop light behind the glass, her hair moving with her steps.
CUT 4 — the longest, calmest part. Dim restaurant, low warm light. She sits at a table FACING a large wall mirror mounted 2.5 meters IN FRONT of her, on the far side of the table; her phone films that mirror straight ahead. The frame shows the mirror with her seated reflection at medium distance, phone in her hands visible in the reflection; a lit candle and a water glass on the table in the foreground. She has time here: straightens her posture, tucks a strand of hair behind her ear, rests her chin lightly on her free hand, relaxed half-smile at her own reflection. Final frame: her calm smiling reflection in the candlelight, sharp and centered — natural thumbnail.

PERFORMANCE
Restrained, self-assured, unhurried: micro-movements only, soft closed-lip smile, relaxed brows, living eyes with catch-lights from chandelier / elevator lamps / street lights / candle flame. Pore-level skin realism, natural skin texture, fine flyaway hairs catching light.

PHYSICS
Hair sways with real inertia on every head turn and step. Dress fabric shifts naturally as she moves and sits. Necklace and earrings catch and scatter light with her motion. Reflections in elevator mirror, shop glass and restaurant mirror perfectly synced to her movement. Candle flame flickers gently, its warm light dancing on her jewelry.

LIGHTING
CUT 1: warm interior key from the chandelier above, 3800K, soft bounce from cream walls. CUT 2: even warm elevator downlight, 3500K, gentle specular streaks on metallic walls. CUT 3: dusk ambient 5600K sky mixed with warm 3200K shop-window glow spilling onto her face. CUT 4: low-key dim dining room — the candle is the key light, 2800K warm flame glowing up onto her face, small pools of warm practical lamps deep in the background, the rest of the room falls into soft dark shadow; intimate hushed evening mood, her face and jewelry the brightest points of the frame against the dark interior.

AUDIO
Live natural sound only, changing per cut: quiet hotel room tone; low elevator hum and a soft ding; evening street ambience, her heels on pavement, distant traffic; then quiet restaurant — soft murmur of guests, occasional cutlery clink, low mellow music bed.

STYLE
Authentic modern phone-camera footage: photoreal, high-detail source, natural color response, true skin tones, very fine subtle grain only, real-time speed in every segment, 16:9 horizontal.

OUTPUT SETTINGS
16:9 horizontal, real-time speed in all four segments.

POSITIVE LOCKS
Same woman from @image1 in all four cuts: identical face, hair, black corset dress, diamond necklace and butterfly earrings. Phone visible in her hand in every mirror/window reflection, phone out of frame in CUT 1. Each segment is one continuous take with one fixed framing — steady relaxed handheld float under 1 cm from first frame to last. CUT 1 lasts 2.5 seconds only. CUT 4 is the longest segment (6 seconds), dim candlelit mood, her face lit by the candle as the brightest point of the frame, wall mirror across the table 2.5 meters in front of her, her reflection at medium distance. Calm light smile as the only emotion. Evening timeline consistent: warm interior → elevator → dusk street → dim candlelit restaurant.
```

ENVIRONMENT

STYLE

2D Japanese anime short film with a hand-drawn aesthetic. Clean inked outlines, flat-to-soft cel shading, simplified shadow blocks, no CG or 3D rendering. Soft emotional storytelling. No 3D render. No CGI. No Pixar-style shading. No photorealism. No comedy. No chaos. No copyrighted characters. No text. No subtitles. No logos. No social media UI. TIMELINE

![日本語](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-日本語-green)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
[CINEMATIC SETUP]

参照画像
@ image1：15秒動画全体の連続ビジュアルキーとして使用。全12カットの構図・カメラワーク・演出・色彩・アニメーション密度・テンポを厳密に維持。
@ image2：キャラクターデザインを厳密に維持。顔・髪・衣装・アクセサリーを全12カットで完全一致。
@ image3：氷の剣デザインを厳密に維持。形状・装飾・青い宝石・氷刃を全12カットで完全一致。

映像：
24fps、日本劇場版フルカラー2Dアニメーション。シネマティックアクション。超高密度線画、ハイコントラストセルシェーディング、太い手描き線、自然なモーションブラー、極端な緩急のある戦闘、インパクトフレーム、スメアフレーム。

舞台：
吹雪に包まれた雪と氷の幻想王国。広大な雪原、凍結湖、巨大な氷柱、古代氷遺跡。銀青の月光。雪煙・氷粒子・結晶が絶えず舞う。

ライティング：
シルバーブルーの月光。氷剣と魔法陣のみ鮮やかな青白い発光。強いリムライトと雪面反射。

テンポ：
P01〜P11は高速ハードカット。止まらない疾走感。P12のみ静かな余韻。

P01（0.0〜1.2秒）
超ワイド。雪原の中央で少女と巨大な氷狼が対峙。背後には氷の王国。吹雪が舞う。

P02（1.2〜2.3秒）
クローズアップ。少女が氷剣を構える。髪とリボンが風になびき、青い瞳が鋭く光る。

P03（2.3〜3.5秒）
ローアングル。少女が雪を蹴り高速ダッシュ。最前列の氷狼へ一直線。カメラが低空追従。

P04（3.5〜4.8秒）
走り抜けながら最初の狼を一閃。狼は瞬時に凍結し砕け散る。青白い斬撃軌跡。

P05（4.8〜6.0秒）
ジャンプして空中回転斬り。複数の氷狼を連続撃破。砕けた氷片が画面いっぱいへ飛び散る。

P06（6.0〜7.2秒）
着地と同時に滑り込みながら横薙ぎ。走り続けたまま次々と敵を斬る。高速トラッキング。

P07（7.2〜8.4秒）
振り返りざまの連続斬撃。狼が連鎖的に凍結し粉砕。氷柱が次々爆発する。

P08（8.4〜9.6秒）
ワイド。残る約15体の氷狼が少女を円形に包囲。吹雪が激しくなり静寂が訪れる。

P09（9.6〜10.8秒）
ミディアム。少女が氷剣を地面へ突き立てる。巨大な青白い魔法陣が足元のみに展開。雪と氷が渦を巻く。

P10（10.8〜12.3秒）
超ワイド。魔法陣から巨大な氷結波と無数の氷柱が爆発。15体すべての氷狼が同時に完全凍結。

P11（12.3〜13.8秒）
インパクトフレーム。凍結した15体が一斉に粉砕。無数の氷結晶が夜空へ舞い、青白い光が雪原を覆う。

P12（13.8〜15.0秒）
静かなエンディング。吹雪が収まり、少女は氷剣を下ろしてこちらを振り返り、優しくウィンク。髪とドレスが静かに揺れる。背後には月夜の氷の王国。ゆっくりプッシュインして終了。
```

### REFERENCE

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
REFERENCE
Use the provided combined character sheet and storyboard board as the main visual reference.

Follow the same boy design, puppy design, bread, sidewalk, low wall, school bag, bottle, warm sunset lighting, and emotional story beats.

Keep the boy and puppy visually consistent in every shot.

Do not add extra characters.

Do not change the core story.

SUBJECTS

Boy: a slim young boy around 9 to 10 years old with short messy charcoal-black hair with one stray cowlick, a narrower angular face, sharp small nose, expressive anime eyes with defined upper lashes. He wears a patched oversized denim jacket over a faded striped shirt, cropped trousers, and worn canvas sneakers. He feels hungry, humble, gentle, and warm. His acting should stay subtle and emotional.

Puppy: a tiny mixed-breed street puppy with charcoal-gray fur and a cream belly patch, both ears upright but uneven in size, a stubby tail, slightly larger anime-style eyes, and a small black nose spot. The puppy feels shy, hopeful, innocent, and lovable. It starts cautious and hungry, then becomes trusting and comforted.

Bread: one small round bread bun. This is the main story object. The boy breaks it into two pieces and shares one half with the puppy.

ENVIRONMENT

Quiet city sidewalk at sunset.

Low concrete wall behind them.

Soft blurred road and distant buildings in the background.

A simple school bag and a small bottle placed near the boy.

Warm golden-hour light with long shadows.

Peaceful, lonely, emotional mood.

STYLE

2D Japanese anime short film, hand-drawn aesthetic.

Clean inked outlines, flat-to-soft cel shading, simplified shadow blocks, no CG/3D rendering.

Soft emotional storytelling.

Warm golden-hour lighting with painterly anime background art.

Expressive large anime eyes, subtle and expressive facial acting.

Gentle cinematic movement, traditional anime-style motion (limited frame animation feel where appropriate).

No 3D render, no CGI, no Pixar-style shading, no photorealism.

No comedy.

No chaos.

No copyrighted characters.

No text.

No subtitles.

No logos.

No social media UI.

No background music, only natural sound effects.

CAMERA

16:9 cinematic framing.

Use close-ups and medium shots for emotion.

Use one wide ending shot for the final payoff.

Slow push-ins and gentle cuts.

Shallow depth of field.

Keep both characters readable and appealing.

Avoid fast movement or exaggerated action.

TIMELINE

0:00-0:02

Extreme close-up.

The boy slowly lifts a small bread bun toward his mouth.

He is about to take a bite.

Warm sunset light touches his face and hands.

His expression shows hunger and tiredness.

He pauses just before eating.

SFX: quiet street ambience, soft breath, tiny hand movement.

0:02-0:04

Medium shot from the boy's side.

A tiny scruffy puppy sits a few feet away on the sidewalk.

The puppy looks directly at the bread with sad hopeful eyes.

It stays still, shy and careful.

The boy notices the puppy and slowly lowers the bread.

SFX: soft puppy whimper, light wind, distant city ambience.

0:04-0:06

Close-up of the boy's hands.

He slowly breaks the bread into two pieces.

Small crumbs fall gently.

The moment should feel like an important emotional decision.

His hands pause for a beat after splitting the bread.

SFX: soft bread tear, tiny crumbs falling.

0:06-0:08

Medium side shot.

The boy gently extends one half of the bread toward the puppy.

The puppy looks at the bread, then at the boy's face.

It is nervous but curious.

The boy gives a small soft smile and keeps his hand still.

SFX: soft hand movement, puppy sniffing, quiet breeze.

0:08-0:10

Low close shot near the puppy.

The puppy slowly steps forward.

It carefully takes the bread from the boy's hand.

The boy stays calm and gentle.

The puppy starts chewing and its expression becomes softer and more trusting.

SFX: tiny puppy steps, soft bite, gentle chewing.

0:10-0:12

Medium shot.

The boy sits cross-legged on the sidewalk.

The puppy comes closer and sits beside him.

The boy gently pats the puppy's head.

The puppy leans into his hand and relaxes.

This moment should feel tender and safe.

SFX: soft fur touch, puppy happy breath, distant street ambience.

0:12-0:14

Close emotional shot.

The puppy rests its head on the boy's lap.

The boy looks down at it with a warm, slightly sad smile.

He still holds his own half of the bread in his other hand.

They both feel less alone in this moment.

SFX: quiet breathing, soft ambient wind, distant city sounds.

0:14-0:15

Wide sunset shot from behind.

The boy and puppy sit side by side facing the glowing sunset.

Their long shadows stretch across the sidewalk.

The school bag and bottle are visible nearby.

The final frame should feel peaceful, hopeful, and heartwarming.

SFX: soft wind, distant street sound, tiny happy puppy sigh.
```

### 16:9の横長画面。一枚の巨大な「ダークファンタジー・キャラクター設定資料兼コンセプトアートボード」形式。

![日本語](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-日本語-green)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
16:9の横長画面。一枚の巨大な「ダークファンタジー・キャラクター設定資料兼コンセプトアートボード」形式。
高品質な日本のゴシックファンタジーアニメイラスト。画面全体を、細い黒銀色の装飾枠で区切った非対称のマルチパネル構成にする。各パネルは別作品ではなく、同一人物、同一衣装、同一世界観、同一のケルベロスを描いた一枚の統一された豪華な設定資料集。
全体構成

画面左端に、縦長の大型パネル。
銀白色の非常に長い髪を持つ若い成人女性剣士の全身立ち絵。夜の青い薔薇園と崩壊したゴシック建築を背景に、黒と濃紺の豪華なゴシックドレスを着て立っている。片手で黒い鞘の日本刀を持ち、静かで冷たい表情。周囲には青く発光する蝶、青い薔薇、青白い火の粉が漂う。

画面中央上部に、横長の大型パネル。
少女剣士の顔を大きく映した美麗なクローズアップ。顔は柔らかな丸みがあり、短めの下顔面、小さく自然な顎。透き通るような白い肌。大きく澄んだサファイアブルーの宝石のような瞳。細く繊細な銀白色の前髪が額と目元にかかる。頬にはごく薄い赤み。静かで無表情に近い、儚く上品な表情。
中央上部右側に、同じ人物の表情差分を縦に3枚並べる。
正面、斜め横、わずかに伏し目の顔。すべて同じ顔、同じ髪型、同じ髪飾り、同じ年齢感を維持する。

画面右上に、横長の大型パネル。
青白い炎をまとった巨大な三つ首の黒いケルベロス。狼に近い鋭い骨格、濃密な黒い毛並み、巨大な前脚、鋭い爪。三つの頭はそれぞれ異なる方向を睨み、瞳と口内は強いシアンブルーに発光する。背中、首、尾の周囲から青白い霊炎が激しく燃え上がる。威圧的だが、過度にグロテスクにはしない。高貴で神話的な魔獣。
中央下部には衣装と装飾品のディテールパネルを横一列に配置する。

青い薔薇と黒い蝶を組み合わせた大型の髪飾り
銀白色の髪に垂れ下がる黒いチェーンと青い宝石

黒い透かしレースの胸元と青い菱形宝石
濃紺のリボン、黒銀のチェーン、複数の青いクリスタル

黒い手袋を着けた手と、金属製の円形刀鍔

刀の柄から垂れる青い宝石の飾り
画面下部中央には、月夜の青い薔薇園、満月、崩壊した教会、青い蝶、青い火の粉を描いた横長の環境設定パネル。
画面右側下部には、小型の横長パネルを縦に3枚配置する。
月明かりの薔薇園で少女剣士とケルベロスが対峙する遠景
地面から青白い炎を噴き上げる一輪の青い薔薇
青い薔薇園全体へ円形に広がる霊炎と魔力の波紋

キャラクターデザイン

若い成人女性。
非常に長い銀白色のストレート寄りの髪。毛先には緩やかなウェーブ。髪は細く、月光を受けて白銀と淡い青紫に輝く。右側頭部に、黒いレース、深い青の薔薇、青い蝶、黒い羽根、青い宝石、細いチェーンを組み合わせた大型髪飾り。

衣装は、黒と深いロイヤルブルーを中心とした高級感のあるゴシックドレス。
黒いレース、透かし模様、青薔薇の刺繍、青い宝石、黒銀のチェーン、リボン、花弁状の装飾を重ねる。上半身は身体に沿った細身のシルエット。スカートは左右非対称で、複数層の黒いレースと濃紺の半透明布が広がる。片脚が見える程度の深いスリット。黒い編み上げショートブーツ。上品で華麗、戦闘用でありながら舞踏会衣装のような美しさ。
刀は黒と濃紺を基調とした日本刀。黒い鞘、青い組紐、金属製の装飾的な円形鍔。刃や装飾には淡い青い反射光を入れる。
世界観・背景

深夜のゴシック庭園。
満月、星空、崩壊した黒い教会、アーチ状の窓、蔦に覆われた石壁、無数の青い薔薇。黒い木々と廃墟が奥へ続く。地面は濡れた石畳と青い花弁に覆われ、月光と青い霊炎を反射する。青い蝶が空間の奥行きを作るように飛び交う。

色彩は、黒、濃紺、ロイヤルブルー、サファイアブルー、青紫、シアン、銀白色に限定する。
背景は暗く低彩度。瞳、宝石、蝶、薔薇、炎だけを鮮やかに発光させる。冷たい満月を主光源とし、青白い炎を補助光にする。髪の輪郭には強い白銀色のリムライト。肌は青くしすぎず、透明感のある白い肌色を維持する。
画質・表現

超高密度、極細密、映画的、豪華なゲーム設定資料集、最高レアリティキャラクター紹介画面、精密なアニメ塗り、繊細な髪の毛、複雑な黒レース、宝石の屈折、濡れた石畳の反射、青い炎の透明感、空気中の微粒子、立体的な月光、多層的な青紫の陰影。
各パネルの境界には、細い黒銀色の枠と控えめなゴシック装飾を入れる。四隅には青い薔薇や蔦を使った小さな装飾。
文字、ロゴ、説明文、UIアイコン、透かしは入れない。

ネガティブプロンプト

低品質、低解像度、単純な一枚絵、漫画のコマ割り、コミック風の吹き出し、文字、ロゴ、署名、透かし、現代的なUI、明るい昼間、暖色主体、赤い炎、オレンジ色の照明、ネオン街、サイバーパンク、過度な紫色、肌の青色化、面長、長い顎、鋭すぎるV字顎、幼児化、過度な巨乳、過度な露出、甲冑、スポーティな衣装、ショートヘア、髪色の不一致、瞳色の不一致、表情差分ごとに別人になる、衣装の不一致、刀の形状変化、ケルベロスの頭が二つまたは四つ、犬らしすぎるケルベロス、丸い頭、可愛いマスコット、炎のないケルベロス、グロテスク、流血、内臓、ホラー
```

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Main subject: Young Japanese couple in their late 20s, naturally attractive with gentle features. Woman: shoulder-length wavy black hair with soft bangs, wearing an oversized cream knit sweater, wide-leg jeans, and cozy scarf. Man: neat medium-length black hair, light stubble, wearing a dark olive hoodie and slim black pants. Keep faces, outfits, and affectionate chemistry perfectly consistent across all shots.
Location: Charming residential neighborhood in Kyoto during golden hour transitioning to blue hour. Narrow streets lined with traditional wooden machiya houses, glowing paper lanterns, small temple gate in background, autumn leaves gently falling, warm lights from homes, distant soft city hum.
Visual Style: Heartwarming lifestyle realism with cinematic warmth and dreamy bokeh. Soft filmic color grading, gentle lens flares from street lamps and sunset, rich yet natural tones, cozy and aspirational atmosphere that feels intimate and calming.
Camera Style: Modern 2020s smartphone video (like iPhone 15 or Galaxy S24) in vertical 9:16 format. Smooth but slightly handheld personal vlog feel with light stabilization, natural panning, occasional subtle focus breathing, warm color shifts, and soft glow from city lights. Feels like a couple filming their own relaxing evening date night.
Timeline:
00:00–00:02
Close-up as they leave their apartment door at dusk. She adjusts his scarf playfully; he smiles and pulls her close for a quick forehead kiss. Lantern light glows on their faces.
00:02–00:04
Walking hand-in-hand down the lantern-lit street. Leaves crunch underfoot. She points excitedly at a street cat; they both laugh softly.
00:04–00:06
Sitting together on a small wooden bench near the temple. They share hot roasted sweet potatoes from a paper bag, feeding each other small bites with loving glances.
00:06–00:08
Back home in their warm tiny kitchen. He stirs a simple miso soup while she hugs him from behind, resting her head on his back. Steam rises gently.
00:08–00:10
At their low dining table by the window. They eat together, clinking small sake cups, chatting and smiling. Soft candle and lamp light creates a peaceful mood.
00:10–00:12
After dinner, they move to the balcony. Wrapped in one blanket, they watch the city lights. She rests her head on his shoulder as he gently plays with her hair.
00:12–00:15
Final shot at the door. They turn to the camera, wave softly with big warm smiles, blow a kiss, and whisper “Good night” in Japanese. Gentle fade with warm glow.
Audio: Soft evening ambience (distant temple bells, light wind in leaves, city hum) mixed with gentle lo-fi chill beats. Natural sounds of footsteps, laughter, fabric rustles, spoon clinks, and warm whispers. Calming and uplifting.
Goal: Create a deeply relaxing, romantic “evening unwind” couple video that feels genuine and aspirational — perfect for evoking peace, relationship warmth, and high shareability as wholesome nighttime couple content.
```

### Cinematic multi-shot sequence, one continuous duel, no unmotivated cuts,

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Cinematic multi-shot sequence, one continuous duel, no unmotivated cuts,
photorealistic, 8K ultra-high-definition, hyperdetailed, 35mm film quality,
professional color grading, sharp focus, fine film grain, depth of field
mastery, tracking-to-handheld fluidity, 180° shutter motion blur.

WARRIOR ONE: a lean older swordsman, weathered face, grey-streaked topknot,
faded indigo kimono with a torn sleeve, katana held low in a relaxed grip,
calm and economical in movement. Same appearance throughout, no drift.

WARRIOR TWO: a younger, broader swordsman, shaved sides with a dark
top-knot, black armored haori over a crimson under-layer, a slightly
longer blade held high in an aggressive stance, ferocious and explosive in
movement. Same appearance throughout, no drift.

LOCATION: a narrow snowy mountain pass at dusk, sheer grey rock walls on
either side, deep snowdrift underfoot, thin dead pine trees, wind driving
loose snow sideways, a bruised violet-grey sky, faint warm light bleeding
from the last sliver of sun on the horizon.

COLOR 60:30:10 — 60% cold slate-grey and white snow / 30% deep violet-blue
dusk shadow / 10% accent of the dying warm orange horizon light and a flash
of crimson from Warrior Two's under-layer.

PHYSICS: real blade weight and momentum, boots break through snow crust
with each step, snow kicks up and drifts in the wind on every strike, cloth
and hair whipped by wind and motion, sparks on blade contact, breath
steaming heavy in the cold.

0–3s — THE CLOSE: wide low shot, both warriors circling at a distance in
the pass, snow driving sideways between them. Warrior One's calm stance
against Warrior Two's coiled aggression. They surge together, blades
clearing in the same instant.

3–5s — FIRST CLASH: tight tracking shot as the swords meet in a shower of
sparks, both warriors pivoting past each other, snow exploding from their
footwork, a beat of stillness as they land facing opposite directions.

5–8s — THE EXCHANGE: fast tracking shot circling the pair as blades trade
in a rapid three-strike combo, blocked, parried, blocked again, snow and
steam thrown with every impact, Warrior Two forcing Warrior One back toward
the rock wall.

8–9s — THE OPENING: Warrior One slips a strike by a hair, time begins to
ramp as his blade angles in toward Warrior Two's guard.

9–12s — THE MOMENT, slow motion 120fps: near standstill as the blades lock
edge to edge, sparks frozen mid-scatter, snow suspended in the air around
them, both warriors' faces taut with strain, breath visible and hanging,
the dying orange light catching the steel.

12–15s — THE BREAK, snap back to 24fps: time cracks back, Warrior One
twists free and disengages with a low sweeping strike, Warrior Two staggers
back a step into the snow, both warriors resetting into stance, chests
heaving, blades still raised, the wind howling between them as the shot
holds wide.

AUDIO (no music): howling mountain wind, boots crunching through snow, ragged
cold breath. On each clash, sharp ringing steel-on-steel. During slow-mo,
sound drops into a muffled vacuum, only a deep resonant blade-hum and both
warriors' straining breath. At 12s a sharp metallic ring and gust of wind as
full sound crashes back.

FORBIDDEN: no CGI energy blades or glowing effects, no floating or
weightless swords, no morphing or extra limbs, no design drift between
warriors, no plastic CGI gloss, no cartoon proportions, no readable text or
logos, no franchise or recognizable characters, no desaturated grading,
no warping geometry.
```

```
Style: Ultra-realistic fantasy, cinematic lighting, magical realism, 4K HDR, soft depth of field, dynamic camera movement, emotionally uplifting tone.

0–3s:
A small girl stands alone in a misty mountain valley. The ground trembles as a massive fire-breathing dragon emerges from the clouds, its eyes glowing and smoke drifting from its nostrils. The girl looks up, fearless but calm.

3–6s:
The dragon leans closer, letting out a low roar that shakes the environment. The girl slowly raises her hand, revealing a single ice cream cone, slightly melting in her grip. Silence falls.

6–10s:
The dragon pauses… confused. Then gently lowers its head. The fire in its mouth fades as it curiously sniffs the ice cream. Soft light begins to glow around them.

10–13s:
The dragon carefully takes the ice cream with its claw-like hand and tastes it. Its expression instantly changes from fierce to playful and friendly. Its wings slowly relax.

13–15s:
The dragon gently lifts the girl onto its back. They soar together through golden clouds, flying above mountains as the sky turns warm and magical. The girl laughs as they disappear into the horizon.
```

### PART 1 (0–15s)

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
PART 1 (0–15s)
Cut 1 (0–5s) — Dressing Room Flashback

Mateo sits alone in the empty dressing room before kickoff. Slow push-in. Match cut into a cool-toned flashback where Elena stands in a doorway.

Elena: "I'm done with you."

She walks away. Hard cut back to the present as Mateo grabs his captain's armband and heads toward the tunnel.

Cut 2 (5–10s) — The Final Begins

Mateo walks alone from the tunnel into the roaring World Cup Final. Crane shot reveals the packed stadium before dropping to pitch level. He receives an easy pass, hesitates, and loses possession.

Cut 3 (10–15s) — The Look

A defender taunts Mateo while he jogs back.

Defender: "Where's your head today, Reyes?"

The Crowd Cam finds Elena on the giant jumbotron. She cups her hands and shouts,

Elena: "PLAY LIKE YOU MEAN IT!"

Mateo looks up. Their eyes lock across the stadium as time slows.

PART 2 (15–30s)
Cut 4 (15–19s) — Turning Point

Low-angle tracking shot follows Mateo intercepting the ball and dribbling past two defenders. Cut to Elena watching anxiously from the stands.

Cut 5 (19–24s) — The Goal

Side tracking camera follows Mateo the entire time. A perfect cross arrives. Mateo performs a spectacular bicycle kick while the camera orbits around him. Keep Mateo fully visible throughout the kick. Cut to the ball flying into the top corner.

Immediately hard cut after the goal. No celebration. No goal-net shots.

Cut 6 (24–30s) — Reunion & Cliffhanger

Mateo is already sprinting toward the spectator barrier. Elena rushes forward.

Mateo: "You came back."

Elena: "I had to tell you something."

Just before their hands touch, a mysterious man in a dark coat steps in behind Elena and firmly grabs her shoulder. His body is visible, but his face remains hidden. Elena turns in fear. Mateo freezes.

Hard cut to black.

End Card

EPISODE 1
COMING SOON
```

### This is handheld documentary footage recorded on an early-2000s consumer DV…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
This is handheld documentary footage recorded on an early-2000s consumer DV camcorder by one of the researchers during an Amazon expedition. The entire clip must feel like real, chaotic, imperfect home video footage of something unexpected happening in the jungle — nothing staged or cinematic.

Four researchers are moving through dense Amazon rainforest vegetation. The lead scientist is a 42-year-old woman in a green shirt and wide-brim hat. Two assistants are men in their 30s wearing khaki clothing and backpacks. The youngest is a 23-year-old carrying the camcorder. All show real physical effort from the heat and humidity and genuine shock. Their clothing remains consistent.

They are walking along a muddy trail when something large moves in the undergrowth. After several seconds of tense waiting, a massive strange jungle monster roughly 5 meters long suddenly bursts through thick vines and foliage right next to them. The creature has dark green and brown scaly skin, long powerful limbs, a thick muscular tail, and a wide head filled with multiple rows of sharp teeth. It moves with explosive force, splashing mud and water.

The researchers react with real shock and urgency. The lead scientist shouts and steps back, the two assistants raise their arms defensively and try to pull the group away, and the youngest keeps filming with the old camcorder in one hand while grabbing onto a vine with the other. The monster continues thrashing through the vegetation toward them before partially retreating back into the jungle. Everything feels hot, wet, dangerous and completely uncontrolled.

The camera is handheld the entire time with the natural flaws of an old DV camcorder: constant shake, imperfect and drifting framing, frequent autofocus hunting especially during sudden movement, lens breathing, exposure changes between bright canopy light and dark jungle floor, motion blur, rolling shutter, mild compression artifacts, faded colors and soft contrast. The person filming moves around while trying to capture the creature and stay with the group, so the framing is often messy and reactive.

All sound is natural and recorded on location: heavy breathing and urgent shouting from the researchers, rustling and breaking of thick vegetation, splashing mud and water, the deep growling and thrashing sounds of the large creature, and jungle ambient sounds. No music, no sound design, no narration.

The final result must feel like authentic, raw footage of researchers unexpectedly encountering a massive strange jungle monster in the Amazon on an old camcorder — chaotic, imperfect, heavy and completely believable as real home video.
```

### Use Claude (instead of GPT) to write your Seedance prompts for better cinematic…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Use Claude (instead of GPT) to write your Seedance prompts for better cinematic prompt structure.  
Generate with Seedance 2.0 (not Seedance 2.0 Mini) to get a large quality boost.  
Avoid negative prompts in Seedance; they add noise and reduce output quality.  
Use a two-part prompt structure: a PREFIX block to lock the look (Style/Format/Lighting/Color/Camera/Skin/Acting/Physics/Composition/Continuity/Technical/Audio), then a Characters section and a Scene section ending with CUT to control edits.  
Specify continuity explicitly (same character identity, outfit, makeup, and location across every cut) to prevent identity drift.  
Define shot-by-shot edits using numbered CUTs with durations (e.g., CUT 1 ~2s) and explicit camera actions (whip-pan, snap-zoom, macro insert) to control pacing and cinematography.  
Include technical capture details like 24fps and 180-degree shutter motion blur to guide motion realism.  
Describe desired camera behavior (handheld selfie energy, micro-shake, quick reframes, lens distortion) to shape camera movement and feel.  
Add physics constraints (gravity/inertia, hair bounce, contact shadows, no floating props) to improve realism.  
If generating dialogue, specify exact lines plus voice style and require accurate lip-sync in the Audio section.
```

#### 💡 Tips

### 用 GPT Image 2 先生成同一人物的角色参考图（三视图+近景肖像），因为参考图会直接决定 Seedance 视频质量，所以要花时间把提示词写细致

#### 📝 Prompt

```
用 GPT Image 2 先生成同一人物的角色参考图（三视图+近景肖像），因为参考图会直接决定 Seedance 视频质量，所以要花时间把提示词写细致  
在角色参考图提示词里强制四个视角必须是同一个人，并锁定五官/发型/服装/年龄感/气质一致，避免生成时人物漂移  
生成角色参考图时用 16:9 横版+电影感人物设定板布局（左侧近景肖像，右侧正/侧/背三视图），并保持三视图背景简洁以便 Seedance 识别  
在提示词中加入明确的负面约束（不要多人/换脸/不同服装发型/网红脸/浓妆/过度美颜/动漫插画/文字水印Logo/背景杂乱）来提升一致性与可用性  
用同一段对话（同一会话）让 ChatGPT 继续生成 Seedance 的 15 秒剧情脚本，利用会话记忆保持人物与风格一致  
把 15 秒脚本按时间段拆分（如 0–3、3–7、7–11、11–15 秒），逐段写清镜头内容、人物动作与台词，便于逐镜生成与对齐  
在 Seedance 剧情提示中明确镜头语言与运镜节奏（慢推、手部特写、书脊细节特写、半身中近景、自然景深、慢节奏不快剪）以稳定镜头表现  
在提示词里固定画面风格参数（暖棕暗调、低饱和电影分级、局部暖光、胶片颗粒、轻微灰尘颗粒、真实摄影质感、50mm 人像观感、轻微暗角）来获得文艺电影质感  
在提示词中明确“不要字幕/不要文字贴片/不要夸张表演/不要商业广告感/不要现代明亮书店感/不要梦幻光效/不要 AI 感过强”以避免跑偏  
在脚本中加入声音设计要素（环境声、摩擦声、书页声、台灯电流感、极轻背景音乐）作为生成时的音频/氛围参考约束
```

#### 💡 Tips

### Activate JSON mode in Dreamina (Normal/JSON toggle) when using Seedance 2.0 so…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Activate JSON mode in Dreamina (Normal/JSON toggle) when using Seedance 2.0 so you can control generation with a structured JSON prompt.  
Use ChatGPT Image 2.0 to generate a storyboard first, then feed each storyboard frame into Seedance 2.0 as Image-to-Video for more consistent, controllable clips.  
Create and reuse a character sheet (fixed face/hair/outfit/style details) in your storyboard frames so Seedance 2.0 maintains the same character across scenes.  
Generate storyboard frames one scene at a time, save them sequentially (Scene_01.jpg, Scene_02.jpg, …), and review them as a slideshow before starting Seedance 2.0 video generation.  
In Seedance 2.0, set duration to ~5 seconds for regular scenes and 8–10 seconds for transformation-heavy shots.  
Treat the first Seedance 2.0 generation as a test run; evaluate the output and iterate by adjusting the JSON prompt before doing final generations.  
In editing, trim the first and last ~0.5 seconds off each Seedance-generated clip to remove unstable frames.  
Use Cross Dissolve for smooth transitions and Hard Cut for sharper scene changes when assembling Seedance clips.  
Sync transformation moments to the music beat during editing to make Seedance-generated motion feel intentional.  
Color grade after generation (e.g., blue-purple for night scenes, warm gold for dawn) to unify Seedance clips into a consistent cinematic look.
```

#### 💡 Tips

Activate JSON mode in Dreamina (Normal/JSON toggle) when using Seedance 2.0 so you can control generation with a structured JSON prompt.  
Use ChatGPT Image 2.0 to generate a storyboard first, then feed each storyboard frame into Seedance 2.0 as Image-to-Video for more consistent, controllable clips.  
Create and reuse a character sheet (fixed face/hair/outfit/style details) in your storyboard frames so Seedance 2.0 maintains the same character across scenes.  
Generate storyboard frames one scene at a time, save them sequentially (Scene_01.jpg, Scene_02.jpg, …), and review them as a slideshow before starting Seedance 2.0 video generation.  
In Seedance 2.0, set duration to ~5 seconds for regular scenes and 8–10 seconds for transformation-heavy shots.  
Treat the first Seedance 2.0 generation as a test run; evaluate the output and iterate by adjusting the JSON prompt before doing final generations.  
In editing, trim the first and last ~0.5 seconds off each Seedance-generated clip to remove unstable frames.  
Use Cross Dissolve for smooth transitions and Hard Cut for sharper scene changes when assembling Seedance clips.  
Sync transformation moments to the music beat during editing to make Seedance-generated motion feel intentional.  
Color grade after generation (e.g., blue-purple for night scenes, warm gold for dawn) to unify Seedance clips into a consistent cinematic look.

### Structure your Seedance prompt into labeled sections (LOOK, CAMERA, SUBJECTS…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Structure your Seedance prompt into labeled sections (LOOK, CAMERA, SUBJECTS, ENVIRONMENT, AUDIO, TEXT) to control different aspects of the generation explicitly.  
Specify ONE continuous unbroken shot (oner) and explicitly add never cut / no cuts to enforce a single-take video.  
Lock the camera perspective by stating filmed POV from a seat in the MIDDLE of the audience and include foreground elements (silhouetted heads, raised glowing phones) to maintain viewpoint consistency.  
Direct camera motion quality by prompting very shaky raw handheld phone footage and never smooth, then describe when and how shake intensity changes (e.g., ramps up hard at the moment of the dragon burst).  
Increase realism by prompting extreme dark-to-fire dynamic range plus volumetric smoke, drifting embers, and real lens flares off the flame.  
Prevent unwanted overlays by adding No on-screen text, no watermark, no subtitles.  
Control audio generation by specifying cinematic SFX only, no dialogue, and no music (add score in post if needed).  
Use @-anchored subject/environment references (e.g., @ is the dragon, @ is the cinema) to keep the main subject and location consistent across the shot.
```

#### 💡 Tips

Direct camera motion quality by prompting very shaky raw handheld phone footage and never smooth, then describe when and how shake intensity changes (e.g., ramps up hard at the moment of the dragon burst).

Use @-anchored subject/environment references (e.g., @ is the dragon, @ is the cinema) to keep the main subject and location consistent across the shot.

### Reuse a character sheet prompt made for GPT Image 2 with Seedream v5 Pro to…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Reuse a character sheet prompt made for GPT Image 2 with Seedream v5 Pro to generate consistent character reference images for Seedance.

In Seedance 2.0 prompts, use reference tokens like @[ref1] for the main character and @[ref2] for the opposing subject to anchor identity/appearance across the video.

Add a detailed camera-direction block (low-angle scale shots, aggressive push-ins, whip-fast tracking, vertical traversals, sudden proximity shifts) to explicitly control shot language and pacing.

Specify motion behavior with concrete descriptors (teleport-like dash transitions, elongated afterimage trails, warped air streaks, blade-light ribbons, compressed shock-paths) to drive “unreadably fast” combat animation.

Iterate by appending a strong final paragraph from a better generation to improve the overall prompt (e.g., “Added last part from another generation”).
```

### Use Magnific alongside Seedance 2.0 to enhance/detail the generated video…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Use Magnific alongside Seedance 2.0 to enhance/detail the generated video output.

Specify one-shot constraints explicitly (30-second continuous take, zero cuts/hidden cuts/transitions) to force uninterrupted camera movement.

Lock character consistency by providing a single reference image for the main character and stating “preserve the exact same [character] in every frame” plus fixed wardrobe details.

Lock environment consistency by providing a stadium reference image and instructing “do not redesign it,” then define time-of-day and lighting (dusk, floodlight towers, wet reflective grass, mist/rain haze).

Control camera behavior by describing lens/FOV and movement (e.g., low tracking shot, ~29mm portrait feel, camera glued to striker, arc to three-quarter front, drop to near-grass, rise and push-in) as a step-by-step shot plan.

Improve realism by prompting for physics and interaction specifics (rigid-body contact physics, real mass/inertia, believable defender reactions, grounded contact shadows, correct mud/turf/water spray arcs).

Set output/style parameters directly in the prompt (4K, 24fps, heavy directional motion blur, shaky-cam handheld energy, no on-screen text, no subtitles).
```

#### 💡 Tips

Lock environment consistency by providing a stadium reference image and instructing “do not redesign it,” then define time-of-day and lighting (dusk, floodlight towers, wet reflective grass, mist/rain haze).

Control camera behavior by describing lens/FOV and movement (e.g., low tracking shot, ~29mm portrait feel, camera glued to striker, arc to three-quarter front, drop to near-grass, rise and push-in) as a step-by-step shot plan.

Improve realism by prompting for physics and interaction specifics (rigid-body contact physics, real mass/inertia, believable defender reactions, grounded contact shadows, correct mud/turf/water spray arcs).

Set output/style parameters directly in the prompt (4K, 24fps, heavy directional motion blur, shaky-cam handheld energy, no on-screen text, no subtitles).

### If Images 2 starts “remembering” unwanted context, start a new chat to reset…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
If Images 2 starts “remembering” unwanted context, start a new chat to reset memory before generating your storyboard panels for Seedance  
If Seedance keeps flipping a specific panel/shot, pre-flip that storyboard panel image to match the direction you want and reduce unwanted flips in the video output  
Leave extra space/ambiguity in the storyboard/action beats so Seedance can interpret the motion naturally instead of over-constraining the choreography  
Switch Seedance to 480p when you want faster/cheaper iterations, and only raise resolution after you’re happy with the motion/camera behavior
```

#### 💡 Tips

If Seedance keeps flipping a specific panel/shot, pre-flip that storyboard panel image to match the direction you want and reduce unwanted flips in the video output  
Leave extra space/ambiguity in the storyboard/action beats so Seedance can interpret the motion naturally instead of over-constraining the choreography  
Switch Seedance to 480p when you want faster/cheaper iterations, and only raise resolution after you’re happy with the motion/camera behavior

### Join the waitlist for Pika Director’s Suite and, once approved, connect Claude…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Join the waitlist for Pika Director’s Suite and, once approved, connect Claude to Pika via MCP to control Seedance 2.0 generation through the Pika agent.  
Create a Pika project and upload characters/references you want to keep consistent across the entire video before generating any scenes.  
Instruct the agent to split your story into batches (e.g., 10-second clips) and require consistency/smooth transitions between batches to assemble a longer video.  
Have the agent generate an image per batch (or a storyboard instead) before moving to video generation to lock in scene composition.  
Iterate by asking the agent to redo a scene, recreate a specific batch, or regenerate an image at any time before final video prompts are produced.  
After approving images/storyboards, ask the agent to generate detailed video prompts for each batch and send them to Seedance 2.0 for video generation.  
Generate all batch videos in parallel in Seedance 2.0 (or run batch-by-batch) and review them in the timeline before rendering.  
Render the final assembled video and finish edits in an external video editor.
```

Render the final assembled video and finish edits in an external video editor.

### Use a character reference image to lock the main character’s appearance across…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Use a character reference image to lock the main character’s appearance across the entire Seedance clip  
Write your prompt as a timecoded 15-second sequence (e.g., 0:00–0:03, 0:03–0:06, etc.) to control beat-by-beat story progression  
Specify explicit camera moves per segment (glides, circles, follows, slow push-in, rises high) to guide cinematography and pacing  
Add environmental motion cues (wind moving bamboo, drifting fog, robes drifting) to increase realism and scene dynamism  
Define a clear emotional arc with visual triggers (terrifying → reveals she’s tending the dead → sadness → cathartic reveal) to improve narrative coherence  
Include a final wide aerial reveal (camera rises high revealing an endless sea of lights) to create a strong climax within a short duration
```

#### 💡 Tips

Use a character reference image to lock the main character’s appearance across the entire Seedance clip

各パネルごとに「全身像」「胸上クローズアップ」「車内運転席」「ドリフト走行」「表情差分5連」「小物ディテール群」など役割を指定して、Seedanceで欲しいカット割りを一枚絵の段階で設計する

文字・タイトル・ロゴ・透かしを入れないことを明記して、生成物にUIやテキストが混入するのを防ぐ  
ネガティブプロンプトで「実写/写実」「単一構図」「吹き出し/文字/ロゴ」「左右対称すぎ」「衣装簡略化」「髪飾り欠落」「パネルごとの衣装変更」「手指崩れ」「車体/車輪の異常」「低解像度/ぼやけ」を具体的に禁止して破綻を抑える
```
